Motivation: 算法作曲作品中，Csound乐谱、乐谱排版、实时控制和排练节拍通常分别独立创作，导致各自的时间线容易发生漂移不同步的问题。

Method: 构建一个单一不可变的有理节拍时间线存储，通过后端特定契约（temporal、semantic、rendering-contract三层）编译生成Csound、MusicXML 4.0、OSC和点击音频产物；时间单位仅在渲染时转换为秒/采样/赫兹；Csound音符引用外部.orc文件中的稳定命名乐器，曲线声明为k-rate信号；节拍后端从同一节拍与速度数据派生并复用Csound序列化器。

Result: 成功实现多后端输出的时间同步编译，并发布了包含所报告输出的归档补充材料（待paclet正式发布）。

Conclusion: 该方案在专有写作环境内有效解决了多模态算法作曲输出的时间一致性问题，但存在与开放源码生态互操作的局限性。

Abstract: One algorithmic composition may require a Csound score, engraved notation, real-time control, and a rehearsal click. Authored separately, their timelines drift. Temporal System is a Wolfram Language paclet that instead compiles one immutable store of typed entities on a rational beat timeline through backend-specific contracts. It emits Csound synthesis, beta MusicXML 4.0, OSC control, and click artifacts that remain synchronized because they share that store. Conversion to seconds, samples, or hertz occurs only at render time. Csound notes use stable named instruments in external .orc files; curves become k-rate signals declared against score p-fields. The click backend derives rehearsal audio from the same meter and tempo and reuses the Csound serializer. We describe the temporal, semantic, and rendering-contract layers, their practical trade-offs, and the limits of this proprietary authoring environment within an otherwise open-source ecosystem. The archived supplement exposes the reported outputs pending paclet release.

</details>


### [139] [The Annotation Bottleneck in Persian Text NLP: Persian as an Annotation-Scarce Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24698)
*MohammadHossein Mortazavi,Mostafa Salehi,Hadi Veisi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过定量交叉验证（W3Techs、Common Crawl、语音语料综述及波斯语-英语匹配对比），论证波斯语并非普遍的低资源语言，而是更准确地被描述为"标注稀缺"语言——即资源生态环境中的标注不足，而非语言本身的固有属性。研究指出标注稀缺具体表现为任务/领域覆盖不均、方案不兼容、获取与文档障碍以及专业领域监督不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将波斯语简单归为"低资源语言"的笼统标签，因为该标签将多种不同类型的资源短缺混为一谈。作者希望提供一个更精确的框架来理解波斯语NLP资源现状。

Method: 综述了截至2026年7月的34个代表性波斯语文本资源，并进行三项定量交叉验证：（1）基于W3Techs和Common Crawl的独立网络可见性测量；（2）从FARSDAT到近期大型语料的语音资源梳理；（3）以Common Crawl网络存在度为基准进行波斯语-英语匹配对比，归一化各任务标注量。

Result: 数据显示波斯语在网络内容语言中排名约前20（W3Techs约0.9%网站；Common Crawl中0.7039%的HTML页面以波斯语为主语言）；语音资源从FARSDAT发展到数百至数千小时的大型语料；匹配对比显示句法和新闻NER标注相对密集，而自然语言推理低于网络比例基线。这推翻了"波斯语全局标注不足"的简单论断。

Conclusion: 波斯语的标注稀缺不是普遍性的，而是不均匀分布的——体现在任务与领域覆盖的不均衡、方案不兼容、访问与文档摩擦，以及专业领域、偏好数据和标准伊朗波斯语之外变体的监督不足。因此应使用"标注稀缺"而非"低资源"来描述波斯语NLP生态。

Abstract: Persian (Farsi) is often described as a low-resource language in natural language processing, but that label collapses distinct shortages into a single category. This paper argues that Persian is more precisely described as annotation-scarce, provided that the term is understood as a property of its NLP resource ecology rather than an intrinsic property of the language. The review covers 34 representative Persian text resources available by July 2026 and adds three quantitative cross-checks. First, independent web measurements place Persian among roughly the twenty most visible content languages: W3Techs reports Persian on about 0.9% of websites with a known content language, while Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30 identifies Persian as the primary language of 0.7039% of HTML pages. Second, a selective speech review shows a long resource trajectory from FARSDAT to recent corpora containing hundreds or thousands of hours of speech. Third, a matched Persian-English comparison normalizes task-specific annotation volumes by relative Common Crawl web presence. The resulting ratios vary sharply: Persian syntax and news NER are comparatively dense, whereas natural-language inference falls below the web-proportional baseline. The evidence therefore does not support a simple claim that Persian is globally deficient in labeled volume. Instead, annotation scarcity is expressed through uneven task and domain coverage, incompatible schemes, access and documentation friction, and limited supervision for specialist domains, preference data, and varieties beyond standard Iranian Persian.

</details>


### [140] [Lost in Speech: Trilingual Spoken Hallucination Detection Across Audio and Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24707)
*Meruyert Aristombayeva,Jason S. Lucas,Chaewan Chun,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多语言口语幻觉检测基准，涵盖英语、俄语和哈萨克语的12,013条新闻样本，包含三种类型和三种严重程度的受控幻觉，并配合290条经过事实核查的假新闻。实验表明基于转录本的检测普遍优于直接音频处理，且合成数据训练的检测器在真实假新闻上有较强的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本幻觉检测已被广泛研究，但口语幻觉检测（尤其是低资源语言）仍基本处于空白状态，缺乏相应的基准数据集和系统性评估。

Method: 构建包含英语、俄语、哈萨克语的多语言口语幻觉基准，通过TTS-ASR管线生成原文本和幻觉对照版本（文本+音频），并收集290条原生真实假新闻用于验证。评估微调多语言编码器和多模态解码器（零样本上下文学习），对比基于转录本与直接音频处理两条路径。

Result: 转录本检测普遍优于直接音频处理，强编码器在二元任务上的性能下降与各语言ASR错误率相关。合成训练的检测器在真实假新闻上有强迁移能力（原文macro-F1 0.82-0.88）。俄语来源分析揭示了与真实性相关的和模型依赖的机器风格信号，量化了合成幻觉基准中的关键混淆因素。

Conclusion: 口语幻觉检测可以借助转录本路径获得更优效果，合成数据训练对真实场景具有良好迁移性，但需注意合成基准中存在的机器风格信号混淆问题。

Abstract: While text-based hallucination detection has been extensively studied, spoken hallucination detection remains largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. We present the first multilingual spoken hallucination benchmark comprising 12,013 news samples across English, Russian, and Kazakh with controlled hallucinations of three types and three severity levels. Samples comprise original articles and aligned hallucinated counterparts in text and audio. We complement the synthetic corpus with 290 fact-checked fake news items collected natively in Russian (225) and Kazakh (65), translated into the other language and rendered through the same TTS-ASR pipeline. We assess fine-tuned multilingual encoders and, in zero-shot in-context settings, multimodal decoder models on transcript-based versus direct audio processing. Transcript-based detection generally outperforms direct audio processing, with binary-task degradation for strong encoders tracking per-language ASR error. On real-world fakes, synthetic-trained detectors transfer strongly (macro-F1 0.82-0.88 on original text), while Russian provenance analysis reveals both veracity-related and model-dependent machine-style signals, quantifying a key confound in synthetic hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [141] [Arbitrary Polygon Oscillator: Generalizing Polygonal Synthesis to Arbitrary Shapes, Morphing, and Three-Dimensional Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24726)
*Antonio Argentieri,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的弧长引擎多边形合成系统，将传统恒定角速度的多边形合成推广为恒定周长速度，支持任意顶点配置（规则/不规则/星形），并通过混合插值算法实现不同顶点数多边形之间的平滑形变，同时拓展到三维通过旋转凸多面体获取连续可变横截面多边形，集成polyBLAMP抗混叠与自适应过采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多边形合成方法局限于恒定角速度和规则参数化多边形，只能在单一形状族内产生平滑音色变化，无法处理任意顶点配置或不同顶点数之间的形变，限制了其作为通用波形生成器的可能性。

Method: 1) 将振荡器改造为接受外部缓冲区中的任意顶点配置；2) 提出混合插值算法实现不等顶点数多边形之间的平滑形变；3) 将范式扩展到三维，通过旋转凸多面体并用固定水平面切片获得连续变化的多边形；4) 结合四点polyBLAMP校正与自适应过采样实现抗混叠，运行时Bézier切线提供几何自适应校正；5) 在RNBO中实现，采用几何缓存策略避免逐样本重算。

Result: 系统在RNBO环境下成功运行，实现了任意顶点数的多边形波形生成、不等顶点数多边形间的平滑变形、三维旋转控制的多边形连续变化，以及无需逐形状解析推导的通用抗混叠方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将多边形合成从规则参数化形状拓展到通用任意顶点配置，实现了统一的弧长引擎DSP管线，为更广泛的多边形波形生成和音色控制提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Polygonal synthesis generates audio by traversing the perimeter of a polygon with a phasor; prior work uses a constant angular velocity, whereas the proposed system adopts constant arc-length (perimeter) velocity. Existing formulations operate on regular, parametrically defined polygons, producing smooth timbral transitions within a single family of shapes.
  This paper generalizes polygonal synthesis around a unified arc-length engine: vertex data of any origin feed the same DSP pipeline. First, we adapt the oscillator to accept arbitrary vertex configurations from an external buffer, opening the possibility for a broad class of closed polygons -- regular, irregular, or star-shaped -- to function as a waveform generator. Second, a hybrid interpolation algorithm enables smooth morphing between polygons with unequal vertex counts, passing through intermediate shapes that have no parametric description. Third, we extend the paradigm to three dimensions: a convex polyhedron rotated about three axes is sliced by a fixed horizontal plane, and the resulting cross-section yields a continuously variable polygon controlled by the solid's orientation. The system runs in RNBO (Cycling~'74) with a geometry caching strategy that avoids per-sample recomputation. Antialiasing combines a four-point polyBLAMP correction derived from runtime Bézier tangents with adaptive oversampling, adapting the correction geometrically to general vertex configurations without per-shape analytical derivation.

</details>


### [142] [SkillForge: Evolving Verifiable Skills for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24747)
*Shidong Yang,Ziyu Ma,Tongwen Huang,Xucong Wang,Renda Li,Yiming Hu,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillForge是一个持续技能演化框架，通过环境交互验证和精炼技能，使技能库在增长的同时保持质量，在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld上持续优于SkillRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数RL训练的LLM智能体是情节式的，无法跨情节积累可复用知识。现有技能方法（如SkillRL）将技能库视为只追加的存储库，不验证存储的技能是否仍然有效，限制了智能体的长期学习能力。

Method: SkillForge通过使技能使用在智能体交互中显式化，让RL同时优化环境动作和技能调用决策；引入基于证据的技能验证机制和多路径技能归纳方法，实现技能库的持续增长与质量维护。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld三个基准上的广泛实验表明，SkillForge一致性地优于SkillRL，验证了持续验证技能在训练更强LLM智能体方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 持续验证和精炼技能库可以显著提升LLM智能体的决策能力，SkillForge为可复用知识积累提供了一种有效框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for complex decision-making tasks. However, most RL-trained agents remain episodic and cannot accumulate reusable knowledge across episodes. Recent skill-based approaches, such as SkillRL, attempt to address this issue by extracting skills from raw trajectories, but treat the skill bank as an append-only repository without verifying whether stored skills remain effective. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a framework for continuous skill evolution that enables skills to be verified and refined through environment interaction. By making skill usage explicit during agent interaction, RL can directly optimize both environment actions and skill invocation decisions. SkillForge further introduces evidence-based skill verification and multi-pathway skill induction, allowing the skill bank to continuously grow while maintaining its quality. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld show that SkillForge consistently outperforms SkillRL, demonstrating the effectiveness of continuously verified skills in training stronger LLM agents.

</details>


### [143] [The RAT: A Unified Bayesian Model for RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24753)
*Pius von Däniken,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯评估框架，通过联合建模检索成功、弃答行为和答案正确性，按RAG流水线的信息流进行分解，用于评估RAG系统中各组件的交互与错误传播。在27种RAG配置上验证了条件分解能揭示边际指标下看似等价系统间的显著行为差异，并分析了标注分配与LLM-as-judge集成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估RAG系统不仅需要端到端正确性，还需要理解各组件如何交互以及错误如何在流水线中传播，现有的边际指标无法揭示这些内部行为差异。

Method: 构建贝叶斯评估框架，联合建模检索成功、弃答行为和答案正确性三个变量，按流水线信息流进行因子化分解；区分任务成功（用户获得正确回答）与生成器是否在给定检索结果下行为恰当；并将LLM-as-a-judge标注作为校准的噪声观测纳入统一概率模型。

Result: 在三个数据集、三个检索器和三个生成器组成的27种RAG配置上，条件分解揭示了在边际指标下表现等价但行为差异显著的多个系统；检索成功标注比任务成功标注对估计策略遵从更具信息量，并从信息论角度解释了这一不对称性。

Conclusion: 该框架为RAG系统评估提供了更细粒度的组件级诊断能力，支持将有限人工标注与廉价的自动化评估在统一概率模型中结合，有助于实践者更准确地理解和优化RAG流水线。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires assessing not only end-to-end correctness but also how individual components interact and how errors propagate through the pipeline. We introduce a Bayesian evaluation framework that jointly models retrieval success, abstention behavior, and answer correctness, factorized according to the pipeline's information flow. The model distinguishes task success. Whether the user received a correct answer (from generator success) and whether the generator behaved appropriately given the retrieval outcome. We apply the framework to 27 RAG configurations across three datasets, three retrievers, and three generators, and show that the conditional decomposition reveals substantial behavioral differences between systems that appear equivalent under marginal metrics. We further analyze the annotation allocation problem, demonstrating that retrieval-success annotations are more informative than task-success annotations for estimating policy adherence, and provide an information-theoretic explanation for this asymmetry. Finally, we extend the model to incorporate LLM-as-a-judge annotations as calibrated noisy observations, enabling practitioners to combine limited human judgments with cheaper automated assessments within a unified probabilistic model.

</details>


### [144] [ExpConCAD: Experience-Guided Text-to-CAD Generation from Shape Descriptions with Implicit Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24760)
*Jingyao Liu,Jinkang Tang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpConCAD框架，用于解决文本到CAD生成中隐含空间约束缺失的问题。该框架通过恢复构造结构和约束范围，检索相关设计经验来补全缺失的空间约束，最终生成可执行的CadQuery程序，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的自然语言描述通常是不充分且不明确的，常常省略了CAD有效构造所需的关键空间约束，而现有方法忽视了这一挑战。作者主张缺失的空间约束应基于底层构造结构并参考可复用的设计经验来推断。

Method: ExpConCAD包含三个步骤：首先恢复预期的构造结构和约束范围，然后为相似的约束范围检索相关的约束补全经验以完成缺失的空间约束，最后生成可执行的CadQuery程序。

Result: 大量实验证明了ExpConCAD的有效性，并深入揭示了构造结构理解和经验记忆在空间约束补全中的作用。

Conclusion: 构造结构理解与经验记忆对于隐含空间约束补全至关重要，ExpConCAD为文本到CAD任务中处理不充分描述提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD aims to generate executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, real-world descriptions are often underspecified and omit critical spatial constraints required for valid CAD construction, a challenge that has been largely overlooked by existing methods. In this paper, we argue that missing spatial constraints should be inferred with respect to the underlying construction structure and informed by reusable design experience. Based on this insight, we propose ExpConCAD, an experience-enhanced framework for implicit spatial constraint completion. ExpConCAD first recovers the intended construction structure and constraint scopes, then retrieves relevant constraint-completion experience for similar scopes to complete the missing spatial constraints, and finally generates executable CadQuery programs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ExpConCAD and provide insights into the role of construction structure understanding and experience memory in spatial constraint completion. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hotjiashell/ExpConCAD.

</details>


### [145] [Linear Probing Provides Robust and Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24780)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Hanqi Yan,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandecic,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了机器生成文本（MGT）与人类写作文本（HWT）在潜在空间中的线性可分性及表征质量差异，提出使用简单线性探针作为MGT检测器，在仅需不到100个样本的情况下即可在4个基准、16个基线对比中实现+11 AUC的OOD检测提升，并揭示了可迁移的共享潜在方向及"机械度"连续谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型的广泛应用，机器生成文本的潜在滥用问题日益严重，需要可靠的检测器。然而现有的监督式检测器在跨域（OOD）场景下性能显著退化，且需要大量且多样化的训练数据，这限制了其实际部署的可行性。

Method: 作者首先分析了MGT和HWT在低维潜在空间中的表征性质，发现二者是线性可分的，并从表征质量（representation quality）的系统性差异角度给出解释。基于这一发现，作者训练了两个变体的简单线性探针，在4个基准上评估其性能，并与16个基线方法进行对比。同时通过探测向量的分析来验证其可迁移性和所捕获的连续"机械度"谱系。

Result: 线性探针在跨域（OOD）检测上一致提升+11 AUC，仅需不到100个样本即可接近峰值性能，显著优于广泛的基线检测器且更具样本效率。这种可迁移性源于探针恢复了一个共享的潜在MGT方向，该方向可跨多种设置泛化。此外，探测向量能捕获连续的"机械度"光谱，可用于AI编辑文本的细粒度估计。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了MGT与HWT在潜在空间中的本质差异（线性可分且表征质量不同），证明简单线性探针是鲁棒且样本高效的MGT检测器，并提供了对"机械度"连续谱的深入理解，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Distinguishing machine-generated text (MGT) from human-written text (HWT) becomes increasingly important due to potential misuse. However, most supervised detectors often degrade out-of-domain (OOD) and require large, diverse training sets. In this work, we analyze the linearity and quality of MGT representations and show that simple linear probes outperform a wide range of detectors while being substantially more sample-efficient. We first show that MGT and HWT latent representations are linearly separable in low-dimensional space, and provide a plausible explanation for this separability through systematic differences in their representation quality. Motivated by these insights, we train two variants of simple linear probes and evaluate them across 4 benchmarks against 16 baselines. Probes consistently improve OOD detection (+11 AUC), requiring solely ${<}100$ samples to reach near-peak performance. We show that this transferability arises because probes recover a shared latent MGT direction that generalizes across diverse settings. Finally, we demonstrate that probing vectors capture a continuous spectrum of ``machineness'', highlighting their potential for fine-grained estimation of AI-edited text. Overall, our work provides insights into latent-space differences between MGT and HWT and demonstrates the potential of linear probes as as robust and sample-efficient MGT detectors. We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [146] [Structurally-bounded Agentic Graph Exploration for Evidence-Grounded Scholarly DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24809)
*Rima Hazra,Sayan Layek,Somnath Banerjee,Soumen Chakrabarti,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Crase是一种有界、可检查的学术搜索方法，替代深度研究智能体。它通过单次搜索引擎查询获取种子论文，沿1.5跳引用邻域扩展，剪除缺乏蕴含支持的引用边，并用考虑时效性的随机游走排序。在LitSearch等基准上，其recall@50比基于专有模型的深度研究智能体高出3倍，成本仅为约三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体采用开放式的搜索循环，推理过程不透明且成本高昂，缺乏可检查性和可解释性，难以保证候选集和停止条件的明确性。

Method: Crase采用固定管线：1) 单次查询搜索引擎获取种子论文；2) 沿1.5跳引用邻域扩展候选集；3) 剪除声称缺乏蕴含支持的引用边；4) 使用时序感知的随机游走对剩余论文排序。候选集、保留原因和停止条件在推理前即已明确固定。

Result: 在LitSearch及另一个基准上（基于500K篇arXiv论文语料），Crase的recall@50比基于专有模型的深度研究智能体高出最多3倍，且成本仅为后者的约三分之一。

Conclusion: Crase证明了一个有界、可检查的搜索流程可以在学术搜索任务上超越开放的深度研究智能体，同时大幅降低成本，展示了透明性和可解释性在论文检索中的价值。

Abstract: We present Crase, a bounded and inspectable alternative to deep research agents for scholarly search. Instead of an open-ended search loop, Crase queries a search engine once for seed papers, expands them along their 1.5-hop citation neighborhood, prunes citation edges whose claims lack entailment support, and ranks the remaining papers with a recency-aware random walk. This makes the candidate set, the reason each paper is kept, and the stopping condition explicit and fixed before inference. On LitSearch and one further benchmarks over a 500K-paper arXiv corpus, Crase outperforms deep research agents built on proprietary models by up to 3$\times$ recall@50 at roughly a third of the cost.

</details>


### [147] [Reading Is Not Using: Retrieval, Judgment, and the Design of AI Financial Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24842)
*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在金融分析中存在"检索-整合鸿沟"：即使模型能准确检索到风险披露信息，这些信息也不一定会影响其投资判断。在2,000到128,000 token的不相关上下文变化下，风险披露对投资判断的影响降至噪声水平，该现象在多个模型和任务中复现。研究发现压缩摘要和源文本查找需联合作用才能将披露信息传导至判断，而工作流架构（分块-摘要管道vs目标化结构化重述）决定了信息传导的成败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM金融分析系统通常仅评估信息检索能力，而非评估检索到的信息是否真正影响模型判断。作者识别出"检索-整合鸿沟"，旨在揭示模型即使检索成功也可能在判断中忽略关键信息的问题，从而质疑当前基于检索的评估体系的有效性。

Method: 实验设计：固定目标公司信息，仅变化不相关上下文长度（2,000到128,000 tokens），测试风险披露对投资判断的影响。使用真实10-K文件移除实验、多模型家族和多判断任务复现，并引入因果记忆干预（压缩摘要+源文本查找）及不同工作流架构（分块-摘要管道vs目标化结构化重述）进行对比分析。

Result: 风险披露对投资判断的影响随上下文增长降至实验噪声水平，而直接检索仍保持准确。该模式在多个模型家族和任务中稳定复现。更强模型仅延缓而非消除此鸿沟。压缩摘要和源文本查找需联合生效，而分块-摘要管道会驱逐相关信息，目标化结构化重述可恢复信息影响。

Conclusion: AI分析师性能由模型能力与工作流架构共同决定。当前基于检索的评估可能认证那些在投资判断中忽略已检索信息的系统，因此需要更全面的评估框架来捕捉模型在长上下文中的信息整合能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [148] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BrowserForge是一个通过并行驱动大量浏览器沙箱在开放网络上生成网页交互数据的框架，生成了包含203,238条轨迹的数据集（每条来自不同网站），比现有数据集更大更多样。用该数据集微调紧凑多模态模型，在Online-Mind2Web上成功率从25.66%提升到33.33%，并一致提升了Multimodal-Mind2Web上的步骤准确率，且收益随语料规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从渲染像素（截图）而非HTML或可访问性树进行操作的Web智能体，训练依赖大量高质量交互轨迹，但现有公共数据集通常只有几千条轨迹且来自固定的窄网站集合，自动化合成管线也局限于预定义网站列表或教程来源，导致智能体见过的不同网站数量难以增长，缺乏可扩展的高质量训练数据生成方案。

Method: BrowserForge框架包含三个核心组件：(1)开放网络数据采集阶段，让智能体接触数十万个真实可访问网站；(2)沙箱集群管理器，调度数百个并发浏览器实现高利用率；(3)Proposer-Solver双智能体循环，将原始页面转化为可执行任务并收集经过验证的轨迹。通过规则加模型的清洗管线移除失败运行，并将推理统重写为统一的链式思维风格。可访问性树等页面结构仅作为合成阶段的信号，训练和发布的智能体纯粹从截图操作。

Result: 生成的语料库包含203,238条轨迹，每条来自不同网站，规模和多样性超过先前轨迹数据集。微调紧凑多模态模型后，在Online-Mind2Web上成功率从25.66%提升到33.33%，在静态Multimodal-Mind2Web上的步骤准确率持续提升，且收益随语料规模增长。对照分析确认开放网络数据来源和广泛网站覆盖是改进的关键贡献因素。

Conclusion: BrowserForge证明了通过并行驱动开放网络浏览器沙箱可以大规模生成高质量、多样化的网页交互轨迹数据，有效解决了像素级Web智能体训练数据稀缺的问题，且数据规模和网站多样性是提升智能体性能的核心驱动因素。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>
